| 2026-07-01 | ERC-7730 follow-ups — Kani re-run (2 harnesses FIXED) + HARD-slice engine DECISION (defer + specified safe subset) | (1) **`make kani`** — the first COMPLETE run (earlier runs were interrupted in pqsigner-tx, never reaching the erc7730 crate) surfaced **2 failing harnesses, both HARNESS-side (not code bugs), now fixed**: (a) `resolve_structured_panic_free_and_in_bounds` (my C1/C2 resolver) was `unwind(6)` but reaches the 28-byte top-zero scan in `read_offset_word`/`read_length_word` (sibling `resolve_array_multi` uses 34) → raised to unwind(34), now SUCCESSFUL (C1/C2 panic-free+in-bounds now genuinely PROVEN, not just declared); (b) `params_rejects_unknown_tag` (pre-existing, regressed by b37a052f) classified "unknown" as `tag > 0x3F`, but `PARAM_CONST_VALUE=0x40` was added ABOVE that block → a KNOWN 0x40 tag satisfied the stale "unknown" assume and the parser correctly accepted it → assertion failed on a harness-induced (not real) counterexample; fixed the assume to the true range `[0x30, PARAM_CONST_VALUE]`. `resolve_array`/`resolve_array_multi` already SUCCESSFUL. GOTCHA: NEVER run concurrent `cargo test`/second `make kani` — they share `target/kani` and corrupt each other's `.symtab.out` (`No such file`, NOT a verification failure); `rm -rf target/kani` + a single serial run to recover. (2) **Adversarial review (5-lens workflow) of the multi-array `MultiInTail` relaxation + Morpho flatten:** MultiInTail **CLEARED** (same-word-both-sides: device+EVM derive the array span from the identical signature-fixed offset word; M4/M6 only widen the accept-set in the SAFE direction — device rejects or EVM reverts, never show≠sign; the `?` at display/erc7730/mod.rs propagates reject → loud blind-sign, not silent-skip). Found **1 HIGH break — FIXED `7c9cf5a2`**: a crafted descriptor with a DUPLICATE tuple-member name defeats BOTH the completeness lint AND mhaas's visibility gate (both name-keyed via position-blind `path_covers_tuple_member`) → an aliased effect-bearing address (`collateralToken`) signed-but-hidden. Flatten-INDEPENDENT (a flat descriptor triggers it identically). Fix: `parse_format_key` rejects duplicate member/top-level names, fail-closed; corpus UNCHANGED (no legit descriptor has dups). LOW record-only nit (not fixed): `array_all_is_multi` naive `contains(0x25)` byte-scan can misroute Sole→Multi, but MultiInTail is a faithful accept-superset → no show≠sign. Residual (pre-existing, separate owner): visibility rule 2 only forces ADDRESS args shown — a non-address effect-bearing arg marked `visible:"never"` still passes. (3) **HARD-slice engine (53-fn DEX hot path) — DEFER**, `docs/erc7730-coverage-blocker-analysis-2026-07.md` §"Decision: the HARD-slice engine". High-VALUE slices (`dex.[-20:]`/`hookData.[32:52]` packed-path) read a **runtime-length** source (position/validity depends on an attacker-controlled length word) AND hide the rest of the sliced blob = array-tail-hiding/slot-confusion class. Bounded-safe subset for a future gated increment: fixed/normalized slice of a **compile-time-fixed-length** source (`bytesN`≤32) → absolute in-bounds range + byte-coverage completeness lint; EXCLUDES runtime-length sources (`bytes`/`T[]`/`[-1]`/DEX). Leverage LOW (almost all registry slices are dynamic-source) → defer-not-grow. |
| 2026-07-02 | SE050 SCP03 idle-relock "wrong PIN" — session re-establish FIXED + send_apdu fail-closed | User-reported silicon bug (`flash-hw-dual-se-lcd-standalone-debug`): first unlock OK; after the 120 s idle wipe the correct PIN was rejected as "Wrong PIN". Root cause: `nsc::zeroize_sensitive_state` → `Se050::zeroize_caches` → `scp03.zeroize_session()` (MEDIUM-1, 2026-06-11) killed the host-side SCP03 session but left `ready == true`, so the next access's `init()` short-circuited and never re-established; `send_apdu` with `scp03.active == false` silently fell back to cleartext CLA=0x80 APDUs which the chip (own session still live) refused — GetRandom SW=0x6982, CreateSession SW=0x6985 → `UnlockError::InternalError` rendered as "Wrong PIN", burning one page-124 pre-commit attempt per retry (~10 stubborn retries of the CORRECT pin ⇒ PinLocked; power-cycle + boot unlock resets). The OPTIGA twin was immune (`ensure_shield` re-handshakes on next access); SE050 never got that half. Same path backs CMD_LOCK → CMD_REQUEST_UNLOCK. No secret leaked: CreateSession failed before `verify_session`, so the PIN never crossed I2C in the clear — by ordering luck only. FIXED 3 layers, hardened: (1) `Se050::zeroize_caches` drops `ready` with the session → next access re-runs full `init()` (T=1' reset + SELECT + establish, = deferred `reinit()`); (2) `init()` treats `ready && !scp03.active` as needs-reinit (covers any future session-killer that forgets `ready`); (3) `send_apdu` fails CLOSED (`Se050Error::Scp03`) on an inactive session instead of the cleartext fallback — invariant #3 now structural (SELECT/INIT-UPDATE/EXT-AUTH talk to `t1.transceive` directly, so nothing legitimate used the plain branch); stale zeroize_session + F8 doc-comments corrected. UX truthfulness: boot + PendSV unlock loops now split `PinIncorrect` ("Wrong PIN") from `InternalError` ("Unlock error") so SE/transport trouble stops masquerading as a wrong PIN; new screen in lcd_render catalog. Source-pin suites (`se050_under_test`, `main_sau_pure_tests`) kept compatible. Verified: `build-hw-dual-se-lcd-standalone{,-debug}` + QEMU `make secure nonsecure` + host suite (exit 0, 2073 in final bin) + `make e2e` all green; silicon re-test = the user's repro (relock → re-enter PIN → expect `[SE050] Init:` + `[SCP03] Session established` + CLA=84 + `[S] Re-unlocked after idle wipe`). Files: `secure/src/se050/{mod,apdu,scp03}.rs`, `secure/src/main.rs`, `tools/lcd_render/src/screens.rs`. |
